# Design planned-work source index

## Status

Retained planning requirements. Design-branch implementation claims do not establish completion of the modular refactor. Unchecked source items remain planned; verify recorded closures before claiming that `main` implements them.

## Authority

The accepted product reference is `design/interaction-polish`; `main` supplies the split-crate structure. This index preserves links to full acceptance contracts at the inspected source revision, including task details beyond checkboxes. Current branch documents may clarify status without discarding these requirements.

## Current evidence

Inspected design revision: `d9e569bf6fd33d90266db597c458442c729340ad`. Each source link is immutable. Counts describe unchecked Markdown items at that revision, not a fresh implementation audit.

## Remaining work

Keep all unresolved acceptance contracts in the linked sources. Integrate shared components and API improvements into focused crates; retain showcase coverage, reachability, responsive layouts, interaction safety, visual testing, and application follow-ups. Source task IDs retain their original meaning.

Recorded unchecked items follow, grouped by source. The source contains the full surrounding contract; these excerpts provide navigation, not a reduced replacement.
